Model before rewriting:

find x: tuple (int(1..2), int(1..5))
find y: tuple (int(2..5), int(1..5))

such that

x != y

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#packed_1: int(0..9)

--

x != y
   ~~> uniform_repr_in_comparison_op ([("ReprGeneral", 10100)])
x != y
new variables:
  find y#packed_1: int(0..19)
